# Wikipedia function list comparison: iOS vs HarmonyOS

- **Source:** Wikipedia for iOS, release of 11 August 2026, build 6144 (upstream tag `releases/2026.08.11`), 1027 functions.
- **Target:** Wikipedia for HarmonyOS (`etspworks/Wikipedia-HarmonyOS` main `3d9029d`), 600 functions.
- Both function lists were extracted from each app's source code.
- ✅ = the function is in that app's function list, ❌ = it is not.
- The function text comes from the iOS list, or from the HarmonyOS list when iOS lacks it.
- 1122 rows: 490 in both, 537 iOS only, 95 HarmonyOS only.
- A note with #N points to the row whose function gives the same result.
- Level 1 Module is a screen of the app, or Global for launch and app-wide behaviour; Level 2 Module,
  Level 3 Module and Level 4 Module are the elements tapped from it, one level per tap. A row ends at its
  own level, so the deeper columns stay empty. An element that opens further elements (a page, alert,
  sheet or menu) has its own row first, with its name repeated in the next column; anything deeper than
  Level 4 is joined into Level 4 Module with `>`. A control of an alert or sheet that no listed tap opens
  carries that alert's or sheet's name in brackets.
- A screen with many functions of its own (Article, Editor, Talk Page, Page History, Settings and others)
  is a Level 1 Module of its own; the row that opens it stays in the module it is opened from.
- A module, element or page is one merged cell over all the rows of its group. A markdown table cannot
  merge cells, so the table below is written in HTML.
- The same tables are in the workbook [FUNCTION_LIST_COMPARISON.xlsx](FUNCTION_LIST_COMPARISON.xlsx),
  with each group as merged cells.
